\displaystyle\bar{\theta}_{pr,+}(\beta^{\prime};n)=-\frac{\gamma}{2^{2}n^{3/2}}\sum_{\ell=-n^{\kappa}}^{n^{\kappa}}\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)(\pi\ell)\sum_{j^{\prime}=-\delta n}^{\delta n}\frac{(\pi k_{j^{\prime}})^{2}\hat{\mathfrak{E}}_{n}(t,2j^{\prime}+\ell)}{\Big(\frac{\ell\pi}{n+1}\Big)^{2}+\gamma^{2}(\pi k_{j^{\prime}})^{4}}

for some
\delta,\kappa\in(0,1)
. Arguing as in (
8.15
) we get

(14.7)

|\theta_{pr,+}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s+o_{n}(1)\Big).

Summarizing we have shown the following.

Lemma 14.2
.

Suppose that
\beta\in C^{\infty}[0,1]
is such that
{\rm supp}\,\beta^{\prime}\subset(0,1)
. Then, there exists
C>0
such that

(14.8)

|\theta_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s+o_{n}(1)\Big),\quad n=1,2,\ldots.

14.3.
Estimates of
\xi^{(pr)}_{\iota}(\beta^{\prime};n)

14.3.1.
Estimates of
\xi^{(pr)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)

Thanks to (
11.1
) and (
11.3
) (replacing

\varphi
by
\beta
in (
11.2
)) we can write

(14.9)

\displaystyle\xi^{(pr)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)=n\bar{\xi}^{(pr)}_{p}(n)+o_{n}(1)\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big).

Going back to the defintion of
\bar{\xi}^{(pr)}_{p}(n)
in (
11.3
) there exists
c>0
such that

(14.10)

(n+1)^{2}\Delta^{\prime}(\ell,k_{j^{\prime}})\geq c>0,\quad|j^{\prime}|\geq n/100,\,n=1,2,\dots.

Therefore that part of the sum can
be estimated by
C\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big)
. As
a result we can write

\displaystyle|\xi^{(pr)}_{p}(n)|\leq C\mathop{\sum_{|\ell|\leq n^{\kappa},|j^{\prime}|\leq n/100}}_{j^{\prime}\not=-2\ell,\ell\not=0}\frac{|\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)|}{|\ell|}\cdot\frac{|j^{\prime}|}{|2j^{\prime}+\ell|}|\delta_{0,t}\tilde{S}_{j^{\prime}+\ell,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}|+C\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathop{\sum_{|\ell|\leq n^{\kappa},|j^{\prime}|\leq n/100}}_{j^{\prime}\not=-2\ell,\ell\not=0}\frac{|\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)|(1+|\ell|)}{2|\ell|}\Big(\mathbb{E}_{n}[\tilde{p}_{j^{\prime}+\ell}^{2}(0)]+\mathbb{E}_{n}[\tilde{p}_{j^{\prime}}^{2}(0)]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{n}[\tilde{p}_{j^{\prime}+\ell}^{2}(t)]+\mathbb{E}_{n}[\tilde{p}_{j^{\prime}}^{2}(t)]\Big)+C\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big)\leq C^{\prime}\big({\cal H}_{n}(0)+{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big)

for some constant
C^{\prime}>C
independent of
n
.
Summarizing, we have shown that there exists
C>0
such that

(14.11)

|\xi^{(pr)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big).

14.3.2.
Estimates of
\xi^{(pr)}_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\xi^{(pr)}_{rp}(\beta^{\prime};n)

We have , see (
11.3
),

\xi^{(pr)}_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\xi^{(pr)}_{rp}(\beta^{\prime};n)=\bar{\xi}^{(pr)}_{rp}(n)+o_{n}(1)\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big).

After a direct calculation we obtain from (
5.23
)

(14.12)

\displaystyle\xi^{(pr)}_{rp}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\xi^{(pr)}_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)=\frac{i\gamma}{n}\sum_{|\ell|\leq n^{\kappa}}\sum_{j=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)\xi_{pr}^{\prime}(j^{\prime}+\ell,j^{\prime})\delta_{0,t}\tilde{S}_{j^{\prime}+\ell,j^{\prime}}^{(pr)},

where

\displaystyle\xi_{pr}^{\prime}(j,j^{\prime}):=\frac{\sin\Big(\frac{\pi(k_{j}+k_{j^{\prime}})}{2}\Big)}{2^{3/2}\Delta(k_{j},k_{j^{\prime}})}\left(\sin^{2}\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j}}{2}\Big)+\sin^{2}\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j^{\prime}}}{2}\Big)\right)\sin\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j^{\prime}}}{2}\Big).

Following the same procedure as in Section
14.3.1
we get that
for
\kappa\in(0,1)

\displaystyle|\xi^{(pr)}_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\xi^{(pr)}_{rp}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\mathop{\sum_{|\ell|\leq n^{\kappa},|j^{\prime}|\leq n/100}}_{2j^{\prime}\not=-\ell,\ell\not=0}\frac{|\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)|}{|\ell|}|\delta_{0,t}\tilde{S}_{j^{\prime}+\ell,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}|

\displaystyle\times\frac{(j^{\prime}+\ell)^{2}+(j^{\prime})^{2}}{n|2j^{\prime}+\ell|}+C\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big)\leq C^{\prime}\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big).

Summarizing, we have shown that there exists
C>0
such that

(14.13)

|\xi^{(pr)}_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\xi^{(pr)}_{rp}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)\mathbb{E}_{n}+{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big).

14.3.3.
Estimates of
\xi^{(pr)}_{r}(\beta^{\prime};n)

Using formula (
11.3
) and estimate (
14.10
) we conclude that

(14.14)

\displaystyle|\xi^{(pr)}_{r}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq|\hat{\xi}^{(pr)}_{r}(\beta^{\prime};n)|+C\big({\cal H}_{n}(0)+{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big),\quad\mbox{with}

\displaystyle\bar{\xi}^{(pr)}_{r}(\ell;n)=\frac{C}{(n+1)^{2}}\mathop{\sum_{|\ell|\leq n^{\kappa},|j^{\prime}|\leq n/100}}_{2j^{\prime}\not=-\ell,\ell\not=0}\frac{\cos\Big(\frac{\pi(k_{j^{\prime}}+k_{\ell})}{2}\Big)\sin\Big(\frac{\pi(2k_{j^{\prime}}+k_{\ell})}{2}\Big)\delta_{0,t}\tilde{S}_{j^{\prime}+\ell,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}}{\sin\Big(\frac{\pi(k_{j^{\prime}}+k_{\ell})}{2}\Big)\Delta^{\prime}(\ell,k_{j^{\prime}})}.

Following the argument used in Section
14.3.1
we infer that

(14.15)

\begin{split}&|\xi^{(pr)}_{r}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big)\ .\end{split}

Summarizing, from (
14.11
), (
14.13
) and
(
14.15
) we obtain that there exists
C>0
such that

(14.16)

\sum_{\iota\in I}|\xi^{(pr)}_{\iota}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big),\quad t>0,\,n=1,2,\ldots.

14.4.
Estimates of
\pi^{(pr)}_{\iota}(\beta^{\prime};n)

14.4.1.
Estimates of
\pi^{(pr)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)

In the present section we show that for each
t_{*}>0
there exists
C>0
such that

(14.17)

\displaystyle|\pi^{(pr)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\Big(n+n^{3/4}|J_{n}(t,\beta^{\prime})|^{1/2}+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)+\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{1/2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s\Big),\quad t\in[0,t_{*}],\,n=1,2,\ldots.

Suppose
that
\kappa\in(0,1/2)
.
According to the calculation performed in Section
9.1

we have

\pi^{(pr)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)=\sum_{z=0,n}\hat{\pi}^{(pr,z)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\frac{o_{n}(1)}{n^{1/2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s,

where (cf. (
9.5
))

\displaystyle\hat{\pi}^{(pr,z)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n):=\tilde{\gamma}n\sum_{\ell=-n^{\kappa}}^{n^{\kappa}}\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)\sin\Big(\frac{\pi k_{\ell}}{2}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\big\langle\!\big\langle b^{(p)}_{z,x}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}{\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(p)}(\ell)\quad\mbox{and}

(14.18)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(p)}(\ell):=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{5/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\frac{\sin^{2}\Big(\frac{\pi(2k_{j^{\prime}}+k_{\ell})}{2}\Big)}{\Delta(k_{j^{\prime}+\ell},k_{j^{\prime}})}\Big(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j^{\prime}+\ell}}{2}\Big)^{-1/2}\Big(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j^{\prime}}}{2}\Big)^{-1/2}\psi_{j^{\prime}+\ell}(z)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(x).

We
show how to estimate
\bar{\pi}^{(pr,0)}_{p,o}(\beta^{\prime};n)
, as the term
corresponding to
z=n
can be dealt with in a similar manner.
By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality applied to (
9.10
) we conclude that

(14.19)

\displaystyle|\hat{\pi}^{(pr,0)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq\left({\cal B}^{(p)}_{0}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n^{\kappa}}|\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)||\ell|\left({\cal I}^{(p)}_{0}(\ell)\right)^{1/2},\quad\mbox{where}

(14.20)

\displaystyle{\cal B}^{(p)}_{z}:=\sum_{x=0}^{n}\big\langle\!\big\langle b^{(p)}_{z,x}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}^{2},\qquad{\cal I}^{(p)}_{z}(\ell):=\sum_{x=0}^{n}\big({\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(p)}(\ell)\big)^{2}.

Recall that
{\mathfrak{G}}_{n}(t)
is given by (
14.2
).

Lemma 14.3
.

For each
t_{*}>0
there exists
C>0
such that

(14.21)

\begin{split}&\sum_{z=0,n}{\cal B}^{(p)}_{z}\leq\frac{C}{n^{1/2}}\Big(1+{\mathfrak{G}}_{n}(t)\Big),\quad t\in[0,t_{*}],\,n=1,2,\ldots.\end{split}

The proof of the lemma is presented in Section
14.6
. We apply
it first to finish the estimate of
|\pi^{(pr)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)|
.

By the Plancherel identity and the fact that
|\ell|\ll n
we
conclude that for

\displaystyle\sum_{x=0}^{n}[{\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(p)}(\ell)]^{2}\leq\frac{C}{(n+1)^{2}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{n}\frac{\cos^{2}\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j^{\prime}}}{2}\Big)}{\Big[\Big(\frac{\ell\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)^{2}+2^{4}\gamma^{2}\sin^{4}\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j^{\prime}}}{2}\Big)\Big]^{2}}.

Choosing any
\delta\in(0,1)
we can find
C>0
such that the last
expression can be estimated by

\displaystyle\frac{C}{(n+1)^{2}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{\delta n}\Big\{\Big(\frac{\ell}{n+1}\Big)^{2}+(\pi k_{j^{\prime}})^{4}\Big\}^{-2}+\frac{C}{n+1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{n+1}\int_{0}^{\delta}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{\Big[\Big(\frac{\ell}{n+1}\Big)^{2}+u^{4}\Big]^{2}}+\frac{C}{n+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{C(n+1)^{5/2}}{\ell^{7/2}}\int_{0}^{\delta[(n+1)/\ell]^{1/2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{(1+u^{4})^{2}}+\frac{C}{n+1}\leq\frac{C(n+1)^{5/2}}{\ell^{7/2}}.

Hence

(14.22)

\sum_{x=0}^{n}[{\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(p)}(\ell)]^{2}\leq\frac{C(n+1)^{5/2}}{\ell^{7/2}},\quad 1\leq\ell\leq n^{\kappa}.

Combining with (
14.19
) and (
14.21
), and
estimating analogously
|\hat{\pi}^{(pr,n)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)|
, we conclude that

\displaystyle|\pi^{(pr)}_{p}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq C\Big[n+n^{3/4}|J_{n}(t,\beta^{\prime})|^{1/2}+n^{1/2}\Big(\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)\big)^{1/2}+\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big)^{1/2}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{o_{n}(1)}{n^{1/2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s+n^{1/4}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/2}\Big].

Using the Young’s inequality (
13.15
) with
suitably chosen
a,b,\gamma>0
we conclude (
14.17
).

14.5.
Estimate of
\pi^{(pr)}_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\pi^{(pr)}_{rp}(\beta^{\prime};n)

Using (
9.18
) (with
\beta
replacing
\varphi
) we can write

(14.23)

\displaystyle\pi^{(pr)}_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\pi^{(pr)}_{rp}(\beta^{\prime};n)=\tilde{\gamma}\gamma n^{1/2}\sum_{\ell=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)\sum_{z=0,n}\sum_{x=1}^{n}\big\langle\!\big\langle b^{(pr)}_{z,x}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}{\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(pr)}(\ell),

with
b^{(pr)}_{z,x}
and
{\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(pr)}(\ell)
given by
(
9.16
) and (
9.19
), respectively.
By the
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, as in (
9.20
), we obtain

(14.24)

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{x=1}^{n}\big\langle\!\big\langle b^{(pr)}_{z,x}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}{\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(pr)}(\ell)\Big|\leq\Big({\cal B}^{(pr)}_{z}\Big)^{1/2}\Big({\cal I}^{(pr)}_{z}(\ell)\Big)^{1/2},\quad\mbox{where}

\displaystyle{\cal B}^{(pr)}_{z}:=\sum_{x=0}^{n}\big\langle\!\big\langle b^{(pr)}_{z,x}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}^{2},\qquad{\cal I}^{(pr)}_{z}(\ell):=\sum_{x=0}^{n}\big({\mathfrak{i}}_{x,z}^{(p)}(\ell)\big)^{2}.

We have the following

Lemma 14.4
.

For each
t_{*}>0
there exists
C>0
such that

(14.25)

\sum_{z=0,n}{\cal B}^{(pr)}_{z}\leq\frac{C}{n^{1/2}}\Big(1+{\mathfrak{G}}_{n}(t)\Big),\quad t\in[0,t_{*}],\,n=1,2,\ldots.

The proof of the lemma is presented in Section
14.6
.

Using rapid decay of
\widehat{(\beta^{\prime})_{o}}(\ell)
and
to estimate the right hand side of (
14.23
) we can restrict ourselves to the case

|\ell|\leq n^{\kappa}
for some
\kappa\in(0,1)
. Combining (
9.22
)
with (
14.24
) we conclude that

(14.26)

\displaystyle|\pi^{(pr)}_{pr}(\beta^{\prime};n)+\pi^{(pr)}_{rp}(\beta^{\prime};n)|\leq Cn^{1/2}\Big(1+{\mathfrak{G}}_{n}(t)\Big)^{1/2}+\frac{o_{n}(1)}{n^{1/2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C\Big\{n^{1/2}+n^{1/4}|J_{n}(t,\beta^{\prime})|^{1/2}+o_{n}(1)\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{1/2}}\big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(0)\big)^{1/2}+\big(\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(t)\big)^{1/2}\big]+\frac{1}{n^{1/4}}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}{\cal H}_{n}(s)\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/2}\Big\}.

Estimate (
13.14
) is then a straightforward consequence of the
equality (
6.6
) and estimates (
14.8
),
(
14.11
), (
14.13
), (
14.16
),
(
14.17
) and (
14.26
).

14.6.
Proofs of Lemmas
14.3
and
14.4

Suppose that
\beta:[0,1]\to[0,+\infty)
is a function satisfying the
assumptions of Propostion
13.2
such that

\sum_{x=0}^{n}\beta_{x}\sim n
.
Then, using (
13.7
) to estimate the right hand side of (
4.1
)
we conclude that there exists a constant

C>0
such that

(14.27)

{\cal H}_{n}^{(2)}(t)\leq{\cal H}_{n}^{(2)}(0)+C{\mathfrak{G}}_{n}(t)

Using the definition (
14.20
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality in the
t
variable we get also

(14.28)

\begin{split}&{\cal B}^{(p)}_{0}=\Big[\int_{0}^{t}\Big(T_{0}-\mathbb{E}_{n}p^{2}_{0}(s)\Big)\mathrm{d}s\Big]^{2}+\sum_{x=1}^{n}\Big[\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{n}[p_{x}(s)p_{0}(s)]\mathrm{d}s\Big]^{2}\\
&\leq t\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{t}\Big(T_{0}-\mathbb{E}_{n}p^{2}_{0}(s)\Big)^{2}\mathrm{d}s+\sum_{x=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\big\{\mathbb{E}_{n}[p_{x}(s)p_{0}(s)]\big\}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\Bigg\}\\
&\leq\frac{t}{n^{1/2}}\Big({\cal H}_{n}^{(2)}(0)+C{\mathfrak{G}}_{n}(t)\Big),\end{split}

by virtue of (
14.4
). This combined with Assumption
2.6

yields (
14.21
). The proofs in the case
z=n
and for

{\cal B}^{(pr)}_{z}
,
z=0,n
are analogous.∎

This ends the proof of (
13.14
), thus finishing the proof
Theorem
2.9
.

14.7.
Proof of Theorem
4.2
in the general case

The proof of Theorem
4.2
follows from the already proved estimate
(
13.17
) and Proposition
4.1
.∎
