2. Proof of formula (
5.17
)

Recall that

(S2.1)

\displaystyle\psi_{j}(x)=\left(\frac{2-\delta_{0,j}}{n+1}\right)^{1/2}\cos\left(\frac{\pi j(2x+1)}{2(n+1)}\right),

\displaystyle\phi_{j}(x)=\left(\frac{2}{n+1}\right)^{1/2}\sin\left(\frac{jx\pi}{n+1}\right),

\displaystyle\nabla^{\star}\psi_{j}(x)=-\gamma_{j}\phi_{j}(x),\quad x,j=0,\ldots,n,

\displaystyle\nabla\phi_{j}(x)=\gamma_{j}\psi_{j}(x),\quad j=1,\ldots,n,\,x=0,\ldots,n,

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}=\gamma_{j}^{2},\quad\gamma_{j}=2\sin\left(\frac{j\pi}{2(n\!+\!1)}\right),\quad j=1,\ldots,n.

We have

\displaystyle\tilde{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}:=\gamma\sum_{y=0}^{n}\psi_{j}(y)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(y)\Big[\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}+\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y+1})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}\Big]

\displaystyle-\gamma\sum_{y=1}^{n}\big[\psi_{j}(y-1)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(y)+\psi_{j}(y)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(y-1)\big]\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t},\quad j,j^{\prime}=0,\ldots,n.

Due to the convention
p_{-1}=p_{0}
and
p_{n+1}=p_{n}
we have
\nabla^{\star}p_{0}=\nabla^{\star}p_{n+1}=0
.
Using the relations (
S2.1
) we can then write

\displaystyle\tilde{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\gamma\sum_{y=1}^{n}\psi_{j}(y)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(y)\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}+\gamma\sum_{y=1}^{n}\psi_{j}(y-1)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(y-1)\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}

\displaystyle-\gamma\sum_{y=1}^{n}\big[\psi_{j}(y-1)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(y)+\psi_{j}(y)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(y-1)\big]\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}

\displaystyle=\gamma\gamma_{j}\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\sum_{y=1}^{n}\phi_{j}(y)\phi_{j^{\prime}}(y)\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}.

3. Solution of the system (
5.20
)

With the notation introduced in (
S2.1
)
we can rewrite the system (
5.20
) as follows

\displaystyle\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=-\gamma_{j}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\Big[{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}(0)-{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}(n^{3/2}t)\Big],\quad j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,n,

\displaystyle-\gamma_{j}\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda_{j}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}\sum_{\ell=0}^{n}[\psi_{\ell}(0)\psi_{j}(0)+\psi_{\ell}(n)\psi_{j}(n)]\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{\ell,j^{\prime}}+\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\Big[{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}(0)-{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}(n^{3/2}t)\Big],\quad j=0,\ldots,n,\,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,n,

\displaystyle-\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}\sum_{\ell=0}^{n}[\psi_{\ell}(0)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)+\psi_{\ell}(n)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)]\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,\ell}+\gamma_{j}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\Big[{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}(0)-{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}(n^{3/2}t)\Big],\quad j=1,\ldots,n,\,j^{\prime}=0,\ldots,n,

(S3.1)

\displaystyle-\gamma_{j}\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\tilde{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}-\gamma(\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{\gamma}\Big(T_{L}\psi_{j}(0)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)+T_{R}\psi_{j}(n)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\Big)-\tilde{\gamma}\sum_{\ell=0}^{n}[\psi_{\ell}(0)\psi_{j}(0)+\psi_{\ell}(n)\psi_{j}(n)]\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{\ell,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle-\tilde{\gamma}\sum_{\ell=0}^{n}[\psi_{\ell}(0)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)+\psi_{\ell}(n)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)]S^{(p)}_{j,\ell}+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\Big[{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}(0)-{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}(n^{3/2}t)\Big],\quad j,j^{\prime}=0,\ldots,n.

Let

\displaystyle\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{x,j}=\tilde{s}^{(\tilde{r},p)}_{j,x}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{n}\psi_{\ell}(x)\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,\ell}=\big\langle\!\big\langle\tilde{r}_{j}p_{x}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t},\quad x=0,\ldots,n,\,j=1,\ldots,n,

\displaystyle\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{x,j}=\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{j,x}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{n}\psi_{\ell}(x)\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,\ell}=\big\langle\!\big\langle p^{\prime}_{j}p_{x}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t}

and

\displaystyle\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}:={\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}(0)-{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}(t),\quad\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}:={\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}(0)-{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}(n^{3/2}t)

\displaystyle\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}:={\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}(0)-{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}(n^{3/2}t),\quad\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}:={\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}(0)-{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}(n^{3/2}t)

We can rewrite the above system in the form

\displaystyle\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=-\gamma_{j}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)},\quad j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,n,

\displaystyle-\gamma_{j}\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda_{j}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(r,p)}_{j^{\prime},0}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(r,p)}_{j^{\prime},n}\big)+\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)},\quad j=0,\ldots,n,\,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,n,

\displaystyle-\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(r,p)}_{j,0}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(r,p)}_{j,n}\big)+\gamma_{j}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)},\quad j=1,\ldots,n,\,j^{\prime}=0,\ldots,n,

(S3.2)

\displaystyle-\gamma_{j}\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\tilde{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{\gamma}t\Big(T_{L}\psi_{j}(0)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)+T_{R}\psi_{j}(n)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\Big)-\gamma(\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{j^{\prime},0}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{j^{\prime},n}\big)-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{j,0}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{j,n}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)},\quad j,j^{\prime}=0,\ldots,n.

Suppose now that
j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,n.

Adding the second and third equations of (
S3.2
), then
subtracting the third one from the second one we obtain

(S3.3)

\displaystyle-(\gamma_{j}+\gamma_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\Big(\lambda_{j}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}\Big)+(\gamma_{j}+\gamma_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,r)}_{0,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,r)}_{n,j^{\prime}}\big)-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,r)}_{0,j}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,r)}_{n,j}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\Big(\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}+\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}\Big),

\displaystyle-(\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\Big(\lambda_{j}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}\Big)-(\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,r)}_{0,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,r)}_{n,j^{\prime}}\big)+\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,r)}_{0,j}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,r)}_{n,j}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\Big(\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}-\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}\Big),

From the first equation of (
S3.1
) we get

(S3.4)

\tilde{S}^{(r,p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=-\frac{\gamma_{j}}{\gamma_{j^{\prime}}}\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{\gamma_{j^{\prime}}n^{3/2}}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}.

Since
\lambda_{j}=\gamma_{j}^{2}
, when
j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,n
we use (
S3.4
) and get

(S3.5)

\displaystyle-(\gamma_{j}+\gamma_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\gamma_{j}\Big(\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+(\gamma_{j}+\gamma_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=G^{(1)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle-(\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\gamma_{j}\Big(\gamma_{j}+\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\gamma_{j}-\gamma_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=G^{(2)}_{j,j^{\prime}}.

Here

\displaystyle G^{(1)}_{j,j^{\prime}}:=-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{0,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{n,j^{\prime}}\big)-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{0,j}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{n,j}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\Big(\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}+\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}\Big)-\frac{\gamma\gamma_{j^{\prime}}}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}

\displaystyle G^{(2)}_{j,j^{\prime}}:=-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{0,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{n,j^{\prime}}\big)+\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{0,j}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{n,j}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\Big(\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}-\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}\Big)+\frac{\gamma\gamma_{j^{\prime}}}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}

The fourth equation of (
S3.2
) reads

(S3.6)

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{\gamma^{2}_{j}}{\gamma_{j^{\prime}}}-\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma(\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\tilde{G}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(3)}.

Here

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(3)}:=\tilde{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}+2\tilde{\gamma}\Big(T_{L}\psi_{j}(0)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)+T_{R}\psi_{j}(n)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\Big)

\displaystyle-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{0,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{n,j^{\prime}}\big)-\tilde{\gamma}\big(\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{0,j}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{n,j}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}+\frac{\gamma_{j}}{\gamma_{j^{\prime}}n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}.

Solution of the system of equations for the covariances

Consider the following
system of equations

\displaystyle-(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\Big(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)},

\displaystyle-(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\Big(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)},

(S3.7)

\displaystyle\Big(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}(\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=G^{(3)}_{j,j^{\prime}}.

By computing
\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}
by adding the first two
equations of (
S3.7
)
we get

(S3.8)

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}{\gamma\lambda_{j}}\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{\gamma\lambda_{j}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{2\gamma\lambda_{j}}\Big(G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}+G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}\Big).

Substituting into the first equation of (
S3.7
) we get

\displaystyle-(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\Big(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\Big[\frac{1}{\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{\gamma\lambda_{j}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{2\gamma\lambda_{j}}\Big(G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}+G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+(\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)},

Hence

\displaystyle-2\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\frac{\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{2\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}+\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}{2\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}

and

(S3.9)

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\frac{\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}{2\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{4\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}+\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{4\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}.

Furthermore, from (
S3.8
) and (
S3.9
)

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}{2\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}-\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{4\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}+\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{4\lambda^{1/2}_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}\Big)-\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{\gamma\lambda_{j}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\gamma\lambda_{j}}\Big(G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}+G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}\Big),

hence

(S3.10)

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\frac{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}{2\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}{4\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}+\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{4\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}

and

\Big(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}(\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=G^{(3)}_{j,j^{\prime}}.

Substituting into the last equation of (
S3.7
) we get

\displaystyle\Big(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\frac{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}{2\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\Big(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}{4\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle\Big(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{4\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}+\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}(\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(3)}.

In consequence

\displaystyle\Big(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\frac{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}{2\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\Big(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}{4\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle+\Big(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\Big)\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{4\lambda_{j}\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}+\gamma(\lambda_{j}+\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G^{(3)}_{j,j^{\prime}}

Hence,

\displaystyle\Theta_{p}^{-1}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j}-\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{4\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle-(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}+\lambda^{1/2}_{j}}{4\gamma\lambda_{j}\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(2)}+\frac{1}{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}G^{(3)}_{j,j^{\prime}},

where

\displaystyle\Theta_{p}(c,c^{\prime}):=\frac{(c-c^{\prime})^{2}}{2\gamma cc^{\prime}}+\gamma(c+c^{\prime})=\frac{2\gamma cc^{\prime}}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle\theta(c,c^{\prime})=(c-c^{\prime})^{2}+2\gamma^{2}cc^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime}).

We can write

(S3.11)

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{3}\Psi^{(p)}_{\ell}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\ell)}

Here

\displaystyle\Psi^{(p)}_{1}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{(\sqrt{c}+\sqrt{c^{\prime}})(\sqrt{c}-\sqrt{c^{\prime}})^{2}}{2\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle\Psi^{(p)}_{2}(c,c^{\prime})=-\frac{(\sqrt{c}-\sqrt{c^{\prime}})(\sqrt{c}+\sqrt{c^{\prime}})^{2}}{2\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle\Psi^{(p)}_{3}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{2\gamma cc^{\prime}}{(c-c^{\prime})^{2}+2\gamma^{2}cc^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime})}.

Furthermore, from (
S3.9
), for
j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,n
we have

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{3}\Psi^{(r)}_{\ell}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\ell)},

with

\displaystyle\Psi^{(r)}_{1}(c,c^{\prime})=-\frac{(\sqrt{c}+\sqrt{c^{\prime}})[(\sqrt{c}-\sqrt{c^{\prime}})^{2}+\gamma^{2}\sqrt{cc^{\prime}}(c+c^{\prime})]}{2\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle\Psi^{(r)}_{2}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{(\sqrt{c}-\sqrt{c^{\prime}})[\gamma^{2}\sqrt{cc^{\prime}}(c+c^{\prime})-(\sqrt{c}+\sqrt{c^{\prime}})^{2}]}{2\theta(c,c^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\Psi^{(r)}_{3}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\gamma(c+c^{\prime})\sqrt{c}}{(c-c^{\prime})^{2}+2\gamma^{2}c\sqrt{c^{\prime}}(c+c^{\prime})}=\frac{\gamma(c+c^{\prime})\sqrt{c}}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})}.

Finally, from (
S3.10
) we get for
j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,n

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{3}\Psi^{(p,r)}_{\ell}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\ell)},

with

\displaystyle\Psi^{(p,r)}_{1}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\gamma\sqrt{c^{\prime}}(c+c^{\prime})(\sqrt{c^{\prime}}-\sqrt{c})}{2\theta(c.c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle\Psi^{(p,r)}_{2}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\gamma\sqrt{c^{\prime}}(\sqrt{c}+\sqrt{c^{\prime}})(c+c^{\prime})}{2\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle\Psi^{(p,r)}_{3}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{c-c^{\prime}}{(c-c^{\prime})^{2}+2\gamma^{2}cc^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime})}.

Formulas for the covariances

Denote

\displaystyle\hat{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\gamma\gamma_{j}\gamma_{j^{\prime}}\sum_{y=1}^{n}\phi_{j}(y)\phi_{j^{\prime}}(y)\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t},

\displaystyle B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}=\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{0,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p,\tilde{r})}_{n,j^{\prime}},\quad B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}=B_{j^{\prime},j}^{(p,r)},

\displaystyle B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}=2\Big(T_{L}\psi_{j}(0)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)+T_{R}\psi_{j}(n)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\Big)

\displaystyle-\Big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{0,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{n,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{0,j}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{n,j}\Big),

\displaystyle R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\iota)}=\frac{1}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\iota)},\quad\iota\in I.

Recall that
I=\{p,pr,rp,r\}
.
Then

(S3.12)

\displaystyle G^{(1)}_{j,j^{\prime}}:=-\tilde{\gamma}B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}-\tilde{\gamma}B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}+R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}+R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}-\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)},

\displaystyle G^{(2)}_{j,j^{\prime}}:=-\tilde{\gamma}B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}+\tilde{\gamma}B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}+R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p,r)}-R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r,p)}+\gamma\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}

and

\displaystyle G_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(3)}:=\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}+2\tilde{\gamma}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}\Big(T_{L}\psi_{j}(0)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)+T_{R}\psi_{j}(n)\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\Big)

\displaystyle-\tilde{\gamma}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}\big(\psi_{j}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{0,j^{\prime}}+\psi_{j}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{n,j^{\prime}}\big)-\tilde{\gamma}\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}\big(\psi_{j^{\prime}}(0)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{0,j}+\psi_{j^{\prime}}(n)\tilde{s}^{(p)}_{n,j}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda^{1/2}_{j^{\prime}}}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(p)}+\frac{\gamma_{j}}{n^{3/2}t}\delta_{0,t}{\tilde{S}}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(r)}.

Rearranging the formulas for the covariances we obtain

(S3.13)

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(p)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\Theta_{p}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\hat{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\sum_{\iota\in I}\Xi^{(p)}_{\iota}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\iota)}+\sum_{\iota\in I}\Pi^{(p)}_{\iota}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\iota)}.

Here

\displaystyle\Theta_{p}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{2\gamma cc^{\prime}}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},\quad\mbox{where}\quad\theta(c,c^{\prime})=(c-c^{\prime})^{2}+2\gamma^{2}cc^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\Xi^{(p)}_{p}(c,c^{\prime})=\Theta_{p}(c,c^{\prime}),\quad\Pi^{(p)}_{p}(c,c^{\prime})=\tilde{\gamma}\Theta_{p}(c,c^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\Xi^{(p)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\sqrt{c^{\prime}}(c^{\prime}-c)}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},\quad\Xi^{(p)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\sqrt{c}(c-c^{\prime})}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle\Pi^{(p)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})=-\tilde{\gamma}\Xi^{(p)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime}),\quad\Pi^{(p)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime})=-\tilde{\gamma}\Xi^{(p)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime})

\displaystyle\Xi^{(p)}_{r}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\gamma\sqrt{cc^{\prime}}(c+c^{\prime})}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},\quad\Pi^{(p)}_{r}(c,c^{\prime})=0.

From (
S3.10
) we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(p,r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\Theta_{p,r}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\hat{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\sum_{\iota\in I}\Xi^{(p,r)}_{\iota}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\iota)}+\sum_{\iota\in I}\Pi^{(p,r)}_{\iota}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\iota)},

where

\displaystyle\Theta_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{c-c^{\prime}}{2\gamma c\sqrt{c^{\prime}}}\Theta_{p}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{(c-c^{\prime})\sqrt{c^{\prime}}}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})}\quad\mbox{where}\quad\theta(c,c^{\prime})=(c-c^{\prime})^{2}+2\gamma^{2}cc^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime})

\displaystyle\Xi^{(p,r)}_{p}(c,c^{\prime})=\Theta_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime}),\quad\Pi^{(p,r)}_{p}(c,c^{\prime})=\tilde{\gamma}\Theta_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\Xi^{(p,r)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\gamma c^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime})}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},\quad\Pi^{(p,r)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})=-\tilde{\gamma}\Xi^{(p,r)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\Xi^{(p,r)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime})=-\frac{\gamma\sqrt{cc^{\prime}}(c+c^{\prime})}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},\quad\Pi^{(p,r)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime})=-\tilde{\gamma}\Xi^{(p,r)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\Xi^{(p,r)}_{r}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{c}}\Big[1+\frac{(c-c^{\prime})(c+c^{\prime})}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})}\Big]=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{c}}\Big[1+\frac{(c^{2}-(c^{\prime})^{2})}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})}\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\theta(c,c^{\prime})\sqrt{c}}\Big[2c^{2}-2cc^{\prime}+2\gamma^{2}cc^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime})\Big]=\frac{\sqrt{c}\Big[c-c^{\prime}+\gamma^{2}c^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime})\Big]}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle\Pi^{(p,r)}_{r}(c,c^{\prime})=0

Finally,

\displaystyle\tilde{S}^{(r)}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\Theta_{r}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\hat{F}_{j,j^{\prime}}+\sum_{\iota\in I}\Xi^{(r)}_{\iota}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})R_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\iota)}+\sum_{\iota\in I}\Pi^{(r)}_{\iota}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})B_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(\iota)},

where

\displaystyle\Theta_{r}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\gamma(c+c^{\prime})\sqrt{c}\sqrt{c^{\prime}}}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})}=\Xi^{(r)}_{p}(c,c^{\prime}),\quad\Pi^{(r)}_{p}(c,c^{\prime})=\tilde{\gamma}\Theta_{r}(c,c^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\Xi^{(r)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\sqrt{c}[c^{\prime}-c-\gamma^{2}c^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime})]}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},\quad\Pi^{(r)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})=-\tilde{\gamma}\Xi^{(r)}_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})

\displaystyle\Xi^{(r)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{\sqrt{c^{\prime}}[c-c^{\prime}-\gamma^{2}c(c+c^{\prime})]}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})}=\Xi^{(r)}_{p,r}(c^{\prime},c)

\displaystyle\Pi^{(r)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime})=-\tilde{\gamma}\Xi^{(r)}_{r,p}(c,c^{\prime})

\displaystyle\Xi^{(r)}_{r}(c,c^{\prime})=\gamma\frac{c^{2}+(c^{\prime})^{2}+\gamma^{2}cc^{\prime}(c+c^{\prime})}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},\quad\Pi^{(r)}_{r}(c,c^{\prime})=0.

Supplement

4. Proof of Lemma
8.1

For a given function
f:[0,1]\to\mathbb{R}
define its even and odd
extensions by letting

f_{e}(u)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}f(u),&u\in[0,1],\\
&\\
f(-u),&u\in[-1,0]\end{array}\right.

and
f_{o}(0)=f_{o}(1):=0
,

f_{o}(u)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}f(u),&u\in(0,1),\\
&\\
-f(-u),&u\in(-1,0).\end{array}\right.

For a given
2
-periodic
f:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{C}
denote its discrete
Fourier transform

\hat{f}(j;n):=\frac{1}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\{-i\pi ju_{x}\}f(u_{x}),\quad j\in\mathbb{Z}.

Here

(S4.1)

u_{x}=\frac{x}{n+1}.

Observe that

\displaystyle i\hat{f}_{o}(j;n)=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{n+1}\sum_{x=1}^{n}\sin(\pi ju_{x})f(u_{x}),

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{e}(j;n)=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{n+1}\sum_{x=1}^{n}\cos(\pi ju_{x})f(u_{x})

Define

\displaystyle{\cal W}_{j,j^{\prime}}:=n^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\phi_{j^{\prime}}(x+1)\psi_{j}(x)\varphi^{\prime}(u_{x+1}).

Recall that
{\rm supp}\,\varphi^{\prime}\subset(0,1)
. We prove that

(S4.2)

\begin{split}&{\cal W}_{j,j^{\prime}}=-i\Big(\frac{n}{2}\Big)^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j}}{2}\Big)\Big[\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})-\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j+j^{\prime})\Big]\\
&-\Big(\frac{n}{2}\Big)^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j}}{2}\Big)\Big[\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})-\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j-j^{\prime})\Big].\end{split}

Here

(S4.3)

k_{j}:=\frac{j}{n+1}.

We have

(S4.4)

\displaystyle{\cal W}_{j,j^{\prime}}:=\frac{2n^{1/2}}{n+1}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\sin\Big(\frac{j^{\prime}(x+1)\pi}{n+1}\Big)\cos\Big(\frac{j(2x+1)\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right).

We have

\displaystyle{\cal W}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\ \sum_{x=0}^{n}\left[\exp\Big\{\frac{j^{\prime}(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}-\exp\Big\{-\frac{j^{\prime}(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\right]

\displaystyle\times\left[\exp\Big\{\frac{j(2x+1)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}+\exp\Big\{-\frac{j(2x+1)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\right]\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\left[\exp\Big\{\frac{j^{\prime}(2x+2)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}-\exp\Big\{-\frac{j^{\prime}(2x+2)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\right]

\displaystyle\times\left[\exp\Big\{\frac{j(2x+1)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}+\exp\Big\{-\frac{j(2x+1)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\right]\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}+j)xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\exp\Big\{\frac{(2j^{\prime}+j)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}-j)ix\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\exp\Big\{\frac{(2j^{\prime}-j)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j-j^{\prime})xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j-2j^{\prime})i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{-\frac{(j+j^{\prime})xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\exp\Big\{-\frac{(2j^{\prime}+j)i\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}+j)(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\exp\Big\{-\frac{ji\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}-j)(x+1)\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\exp\Big\{\frac{ji\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j-j^{\prime})(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\exp\Big\{-\frac{ji\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{-\frac{(j+j^{\prime})(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\exp\Big\{\frac{ji\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}+j)(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}+j)(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}-j)(x+1)\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{1/2}}{2(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}-j)(x+1)\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j-j^{\prime})(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{1/2}}{2(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j-j^{\prime})(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2i(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{-\frac{(j+j^{\prime})(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2(n+1)}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\sum_{x=0}^{n}\exp\Big\{-\frac{(j+j^{\prime})(x+1)i\pi}{n+1}\Big\}\varphi^{\prime}\left(\frac{x+1}{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle=-n^{1/2}i\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}+j)xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}\left(\frac{x}{n+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-n^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}+j)xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}\left(\frac{x}{n+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+n^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\Big[\varphi^{\prime}\left(0\right)-(-1)^{j+j^{\prime}}\varphi^{\prime}\left(1\right)\right)\Big]

\displaystyle+n^{1/2}i\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j-j^{\prime})xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}\left(\frac{x}{n+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+n^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}-j)xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}\left(\frac{x}{n+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-n^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\Big[\varphi^{\prime}\left(0\right)-(-1)^{j-j^{\prime}}\varphi^{\prime}\left(1\right)\right)\Big]

\displaystyle=-n^{1/2}i\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}+j)xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}\left(\frac{x}{n+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-n^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}+j)xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}\left(\frac{x}{n+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+n^{1/2}i\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j-j^{\prime})xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}\left(\frac{x}{n+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+n^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{j\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)\left(\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\sum_{x=-n-1}^{n}\exp\Big\{\frac{(j^{\prime}-j)xi\pi}{n+1}\Big\}(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}\left(\frac{x}{n+1}\right)\right)

Here

\varphi_{e}(u)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\varphi(u),&u\in[0,1],\\
&\\
\varphi(-u),&u\in[-1,0]\end{array}\right.

and

\varphi_{o}(u)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\varphi(u),&u\in[0,1],\\
&\\
-\varphi(-u),&u\in[-1,0).\end{array}\right.

We have concluded therefore formula (
S4.2
).

5. Proof of formula (
8.6
)

Using formula (
6.7
) we arrive at

(S5.1)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n+1}\theta_{pr}(\varphi^{\prime};n)=-\frac{1}{n+1}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{n}{\cal W}_{j,j^{\prime}}\Theta_{p,r}^{(1)}(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})F_{j,j^{\prime}}\quad\mbox{with}

\displaystyle\Theta_{p,r}^{(1)}(c,c^{\prime})=\sqrt{cc^{\prime}}\Theta_{p,r}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{(c-c^{\prime})c^{\prime}\sqrt{c}}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle F_{j,j^{\prime}}=\gamma\sum_{y=1}^{n}\phi_{j}(y)\phi_{j^{\prime}}(y)\big\langle\!\big\langle(\nabla^{\star}p_{y})^{2}\big\rangle\!\big\rangle_{t},

\displaystyle{\cal W}_{j,j^{\prime}}:=n^{1/2}\sum_{x=0}^{n}\phi_{j^{\prime}}(x+1)\psi_{j}(x)\varphi^{\prime}(u_{x+1}),

\displaystyle\Pi^{(pr)}_{p}(c,c^{\prime})=\tilde{\gamma}\Theta_{pr}(c,c^{\prime}),\quad\Theta_{pr}(c,c^{\prime})=\frac{(c-c^{\prime})\sqrt{c^{\prime}}}{\theta(c,c^{\prime})},

\displaystyle{\cal W}_{j,j^{\prime}}=-i\Big(\frac{n}{2}\Big)^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\cos\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j}}{2}\Big)\Big[\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})-\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j+j^{\prime})\Big]

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{n}{2}\Big)^{1/2}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{0,j}}{2}\right)^{1/2}\sin\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j}}{2}\Big)\Big[\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})-\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j-j^{\prime})\Big]

and

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}=4\sin^{2}\left(\frac{\pi j}{2(n+1)}\right),\quad\psi_{j}(x)=\left(\frac{2-\delta_{0,j}}{n+1}\right)^{1/2}\cos\left(\frac{\pi j(2x+1)}{2(n+1)}\right),

\displaystyle\phi_{j}(x)=\left(\frac{2}{n+1}\right)^{1/2}\sin\left(\frac{jx\pi}{n+1}\right),\quad\lambda_{j},\quad x,j=1,\ldots,n.

Subsituting for
{\cal W}_{j,j^{\prime}}
we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n+1}\theta_{pr}(\varphi^{\prime};n)=\frac{1}{n+1}\theta_{pr}^{(o)}(\varphi^{\prime};n)+\frac{1}{n+1}\theta_{pr}^{(e)}(\varphi^{\prime};n),\quad\mbox{where}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n+1}\theta_{pr}^{(o)}(\varphi^{\prime};n)=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{n}\Big[\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})-\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j+j^{\prime})\Big]\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n+1}\theta_{pr}^{(e)}(\varphi^{\prime};n)=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{n}\Big[\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})-\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j-j^{\prime})\Big]\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

where

\displaystyle\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})=\frac{\cos\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j}}{2}\Big)(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\gamma_{j}}{\theta(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})}=\frac{\sin(\pi k_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\lambda_{j^{\prime}}}{\theta(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})},

\displaystyle\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})=\frac{\sin\Big(\frac{\pi k_{j}}{2}\Big)(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\gamma_{j}}{\theta(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})}=\frac{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j^{\prime}})\lambda_{j^{\prime}}\lambda_{j}}{2\theta(\lambda_{j},\lambda_{j^{\prime}})}.

Taking into accound parity
F_{-j,j^{\prime}}=F_{j,-j^{\prime}}=-F_{j,j^{\prime}}

and the fact that

\displaystyle\cos\Big(\frac{-(n+1)\pi}{2(n+1)}\Big)=\phi_{0}(n)=\phi_{-n-1}(n+1)=\phi_{n+1}(n)=0

we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n+1}\theta_{pr}^{(o)}(\varphi^{\prime};n)=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{n}\Big[\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})-\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j+j^{\prime})\Big]\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle-\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{-1}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Big]\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,-j^{\prime})F_{j,-j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{-1}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Big]\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=-n-1}^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(-j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(-j,j^{\prime})F_{-j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=-n-1}^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(-j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(-j,-j^{\prime})F_{-j,-j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=-n-1}^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{in^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{o}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(o)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}.

Likewise

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n+1}\theta_{pr}^{(e)}(\varphi^{\prime};n)=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{n}\Big[\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})-\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j-j^{\prime})\Big]\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{-1}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,-j^{\prime})F_{j,-j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{1/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=0}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=-n-1}^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(-j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(-j,j^{\prime})F_{-j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=0}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=-n-1}^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(-j-j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(-j,-j^{\prime})F_{-j,-j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=0}^{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j=-n-1}^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j^{\prime},j)F_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{n^{1/2}}{2^{3/2}(n+1)}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=-n-1}^{n}\widehat{(\varphi^{\prime})_{e}}(j+j^{\prime})\Theta_{pr}^{(e)}(j,j^{\prime})F_{j,j^{\prime}}.

It proves that

\displaystyle\theta_{pr}^{(e)}(\varphi^{\prime};n)=0.
